local function Fifigarebokes(Jipakigukebiqim,Fozogucog)
  local Pitovuf={}
  for Carageda=1,#Jipakigukebiqim do Pitovuf[Carageda]=string.char(Jipakigukebiqim:byte(Carageda)-Fozogucog)
  return table.concat(Pitovuf)
end
